(!rawScanId) {
      return new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: "raw_scan_id is required" }), {
        status: 400,
        headers: { ...corsHeaders, "Content-Type": "application/json" },
      });
    }

    console.log(`[Bootstrap-Environment] Processing raw_scan_id: ${rawScanId}`);

    // 读取原始扫描记录
    const { data: rawScan, error: fetchError } = await supabase
      .from("raw_environment_scans")
      .select("*")
      .eq("id", rawScanId)
      .eq("user_id", user.id)
      .maybeSingle();

    if (fetchError || !rawScan) {
      return new Response(
        JSON.stringify({ error: fetchError?.message || "Raw scan not found" }),
        { status: 404, headers: { ...corsHeaders, "Content-Type": "application/json" } }
      );
    }

    // 执行推理
    const profileDraft = buildProfileDraft(rawScan);

    // 存入 environment_profiles
    const supabaseService = createClient(
      Deno.env.get("SUPABASE_URL") ?? "",
      Deno.env.get("SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY") ?? ""
    );

    const { data: profile, error: insertError } = await supabaseService
      .from("environment_profiles")
      .insert({
        url: profileDraft.url,
        environment_type: profileDraft.environment_type,
        perception_surfaces: profileDraft.perception_surfaces,
        execution_surfaces: profileDraft.execution_surfaces,
        feedback_surfaces: profileDraft.feedback_surfaces,
        recommended_adapters: profileDraft.recommended_adapters,
        missing_capabilities: profileDraft.missing_capabilities,
        elements: profileDraft.elements,
        raw_scan_id: rawScanId,
        raw_profile: { source: "bootstrap-environment", raw_scan_id: rawScanId },
        scan_status: "success",
        user_id: user.id,
      })
      .select()
      .maybeSingle();

    if (insertError) {
      console.error("[Bootstrap-Environment] Insert error:", insertError);
      return new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: insertError.message }), {
        status: 500,
        headers: { ...corsHeaders, "Content-Type": "application/json" },
      });
    }

    return new Response(
      JSON.stringify({ data: { profile, draft: profileDraft } }),
      { status: 200, headers: { ...corsHeaders, "Content-Type": "application/json" } }
    );

  } catch (error) {
    console.error("[Bootstrap-Environment] Error:", error);
    return new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: (error as Error).message }), {
      status: 500,
      headers: { ...corsHeaders, "Content-Type": "application/json" },
    });
  }
});

```

New String: ```
  // ── 扫描操作 ─ Bootloader → Raw Scan → Bootstrap-Environment → Profile ─
  const scan = async () => {
    if (!url.trim()) { toast.error('请输入目标URL'); return; }
    if (!user) { toast.error('请先登录'); return; }

    setScanning(true);
    setSaved(false);
    setResult(null);
    setBootStep('scanning');

    try {
      const session = await supabase.auth.getSession();
      const token = session.data.session?.access_token;
      if (!token) throw new Error('未获取登录 token');

      const supabaseUrl = Deno.env.get('SUPABASE_URL');

      // Step 1: 调用 Bootloader (最小扫描)
      const bootRes = await fetch(`${supabaseUrl}/functions/v1/bootstrap-env`, {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: {
          'Authorization': `Bearer ${token}`,
          'Content-Type': 'application/json',
        },
        body: JSON.stringify({ target_url: url.trim() }),
      });

      if (!bootRes.ok) {
        const err = await bootRes.json().catch(() => ({}));
        throw new Error(err.error || `Bootloader 失败: ${bootRes.status}`);
      }

      const bootData = await bootRes.json();
      const rawScanId = bootData.data?.raw_scan_id;
      if (!rawScanId) throw new Error('Bootloader 未返回 raw_scan_id');

      toast.success(`原始扫描完成，共发现 ${bootData.data?.raw_elements?.length || 0} 个元素`);

      // Step 2: 调用白质层推理 (bootstrap-environment)
      setBootStep('analyzing');

      const envRes = await fetch(`${supabaseUrl}/functions/v1/bootstrap-environment`, {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: {
          'Authorization': `Bearer ${token}`,
          'Content-Type': 'application/json',
        },
        body: JSON.stringify({ raw_scan_id: rawScanId }),
      });

      if (!envRes.ok) {
        const err = await envRes.json().catch(() => ({}));
        throw new Error(err.error || `推理失败: ${envRes.status}`);
      }

      const envData = await envRes.json();
      const profile = envData.data?.profile;
      if (!profile) throw new Error('推理未返回 profile');

      setResult(profile);
      setSaved(true);
      toast.success('环境能力画像生成完成，已保存至记忆库');
      fetchHistory();
      setBootStep('done');

    } catch (err: any) {
      console.error('Scan error:', err);
      toast.error(err.message || '扫描失败');
      setBootStep('idle');
    } finally {
      setScanning(false);
    }
  };

NAME: supabase_deploy_edge_function
ARGUMENTS: {'name': 'bootstrap-environment', 'appId': 'app-br1wyyyd6dq9', 'userId': 'user-bm08r8ttvif4', 'appType': 'Web', 'files': [{'path': '/workspace/app-br1wyyyd6dq9/supabase/functions/bootstrap-environment/index.ts', 'name': 'index.ts', 'content': 'import { createClient } from "npm:@supabase/supabase-js@2";\n\nconst corsHeaders = {\n  "Access-Control-Allow-Origin": "*",\n  "Access-Control-Allow-Headers": "authorization, x-client-info, apikey, content-type",\n};\n\n/**\n * 白质层 Bootstrap Environment — 负责复杂推理\n * \n * 输入: raw_scan_id (或直接传入 raw_scan 对象)\n * 输出: environment_profile 草案\n * \n * 负责：\n * - 判断 perception_surfaces / execution_surfaces / feedback_surfaces\n * - 生成 elements 语义角色\n * - 推荐 adapters\n * - 给出 risk_level\n * - 给出 selector 候选和 selector\n */\n\n// ─── Selector 生成优先级 ─────────────────────────────────\ntype SelectorCandidate = { selector: string; strategy: string; score: number };\n\nfunction buildSelectorCandidates(el: any): SelectorCandidate[] {\n  const candidates: SelectorCandidate[] = [];\n\n  // 1. data-testid / data-test / data-cy\n  if (el["data-testid"]) {\n    candidates.push({ selector: `[data-testid="${el["data-testid"]}"]`, strategy: "data-testid", score: 100 });\n  }\n  if (el["data-test"]) {\n    candidates.push({ selector: `[data-test="${el["data-test"]}"]`, strategy: "data-test", score: 95 });\n  }\n  if (el["data-cy"]) {\n    candidates.push({ selector: `[data-cy="${el["data-cy"]}"]`, strategy: "data-cy", score: 95 });\n  }\n\n  // 2. aria-label\n  if (el["aria-label"]) {\n    candidates.push({ selector: `[aria-label="${el["aria-label"]}"]`, strategy: "aria-label", score: 90 });\n  }\n\n  // 3. role + accessible name\n  if (el.role && el.title) {\n    candidates.push({ selector: `[role="${el.role}"][title="${el.title}"]`, strategy: "role+title", score: 85 });\n  }\n\n  // 4. label 对应 input (简化：用 id 推断)\n  if (el.id) {\n    candidates.push({ selector: `#${el.id}`, strategy: "id", score: 80 });\n  }\n\n  // 5. name\n  if (el.name) {\n    candidates.push({ selector: `${el.tag}[name="${el.name}"]`, strategy: "name", score: 75 });\n  }\n\n  // 6. placeholder\n  if (el.placeholder) {\n    candidates.push({ selector: `[placeholder="${el.placeholder}"]`, strategy: "placeholder", score: 70 });\n  }\n\n  // 7. text\n  if (el.text && el.text.length > 0 && el.text.length < 50) {\n    candidates.push({ selector: `text="${el.text}"`, strategy: "text", score: 60 });\n  }\n\n  // 8. CSS fallback\n  if (el.class) {\n    const classes = el.class\n      .split(/\\s+/)\n      .filter((c: string) => c && !c.includes(\'hover\') && !c.includes(\'active\'))\n      .join(\'.\');\n    if (classes) {\n      candidates.push({ selector: `${el.tag}.${classes}`, strategy: "css-class", score: 40 });\n    }\n  }\n\n  // 9. tag fallback\n  candidates.push({ selector: el.tag, strategy: "tag", score: 10 });\n\n  // 排序：分数高的在前\n  return candidates.sort((a, b) => b.score - a.score);\n}\n\nfunction pickBestSelector(candidates: SelectorCandidate[]): { selector: string; stable_score: number } {\n  if (candidates.length === 0) return { selector: "", stable_score: 0 };\n  return {\n    selector: candidates[0].selector,\n    stable_score: candidates[0].score,\n  };\n}\n\n// ─── Risk Level 推断 ─────────────────────────────────\nfunction inferRiskLevel(el: any): string {\n  const text = (el.text || el["aria-label"] || el.placeholder || "").toLowerCase();\n\n  // forbidden/high: 敏感操作\n  if (\n    /(删除|移除|delete|remove|pay|purchase|transfer|authorize|修改密码|支付|购买|转账|授权|清空|clear|drop|destroy|trash)/.test(text)\n  ) {\n    return "high";\n  }\n\n  // medium: 状态变更操作\n  if (\n    /(submit|login|send|save|update|confirm|apply|登录|发送|提交|保存|更新|确认)/.test(text)\n  ) {\n    return "medium";\n  }\n\n  return "low";\n}\n\n// ─── Action Candidates 推断 ─────────────────────────────────\nfunction inferActionCandidates(el: any): string[] {\n  const tag = el.tag;\n  const type = el.type;\n\n  if (tag === "button" || tag === "a") return ["click", "hover"];\n  if (tag === "input" && type !== "submit" && type !== "button") {\n    return ["fill", "click", "focus", "clear"];\n  }\n  if (tag === "textarea") return ["fill", "click", "focus", "clear"];\n  if (tag === "select") return ["select", "click", "focus"];\n  if (tag === "form") return ["submit"];\n  return ["click"];\n}\n\n// ─── 语义角色推断 ─────────────────────────────────\nfunction inferSemanticRole(el: any): string {\n  const tag = el.tag;\n  const type = el.type;\n  const text = (el.text || "").toLowerCase();\n\n  if (tag === "input") {\n    if (type === "password") return "password_input";\n    if (type === "email") return "email_input";\n    if (type === "search") return "search_input";\n    if (type === "checkbox") return "checkbox";\n    if (type === "radio") return "radio";\n    if (type === "file") return "file_input";\n    if (type === "submit" || type === "button") return "submit_button";\n    return "text_input";\n  }\n  if (tag === "textarea") return "text_area";\n  if (tag === "select") return "dropdown";\n  if (tag === "button") {\n    if (text.includes("submit") || text.includes("提交")) return "submit_button";\n    if (text.includes("login") || text.includes("登录")) return "login_button";\n    if (text.includes("save") || text.includes("保存")) return "save_button";\n    if (text.includes("delete") || text.includes("删除")) return "delete_button";\n    if (text.includes("cancel") || text.includes("取消")) return "cancel_button";\n    return "action_button";\n  }\n  if (tag === "a") return "link";\n  if (tag === "form") return "form_container";\n  if (tag === "dialog") return "modal";\n  return "generic_element";\n}\n\n// ─── Surfaces 判断 ─────────────────────────────────\nfunction deriveSurfaces(rawElements: any[]) {\n  const hasInput = rawElements.some(e => e.tag === "input" || e.tag === "textarea");\n  const hasButton = rawElements.some(e => e.tag === "button" || e.tag === "a");\n  const hasSelect = rawElements.some(e => e.tag === "select");\n  const hasForm = rawElements.some(e => e.tag === "form");\n  const hasDialog = rawElements.some(e => e.tag === "dialog" || e.role === "dialog");\n\n  const perception_surfaces = ["dom", "url", "title", "visible_text"];\n  const execution_surfaces = ["wait", "screenshot", "navigate", "press_key"];\n  const feedback_surfaces = ["url_change", "dom_change", "network_idle"];\n\n  if (hasInput || hasSelect) {\n    perception_surfaces.push("form_fields");\n    execution_surfaces.push("fill");\n    feedback_surfaces.push("validation_error");\n  }\n  if (hasButton) {\n    perception_surfaces.push("button_labels", "link_texts");\n    execution_surfaces.push("click");\n    feedback_surfaces.push("element_visible", "element_hidden");\n  }\n  if (hasSelect) {\n    execution_surfaces.push("select");\n  }\n  if (hasDialog) {\n    perception_surfaces.push("modal_content");\n    feedback_surfaces.push("toast_or_alert");\n  }\n\n  // 去重\n  return {\n    perception_surfaces: [...new Set(perception_surfaces)],\n    execution_surfaces: [...new Set(execution_surfaces)],\n    feedback_surfaces: [...new Set(feedback_surfaces)],\n  };\n}\n\n// ─── Adapters 推荐 ─────────────────────────────────\nfunction recommendAdapters(surfaces: {\n  perception_surfaces: string[];\n  execution_surfaces: string[];\n  feedback_surfaces: string[];\n}): string[] {\n  const adapters = new Set<string>(["dom_reader"]);\n\n  if (surfaces.execution_surfaces.includes("click")) adapters.add("click_adapter");\n  if (surfaces.execution_surfaces.includes("fill")) adapters.add("fill_adapter");\n  if (surfaces.execution_surfaces.includes("select")) adapters.add("select_adapter");\n  if (surfaces.execution_surfaces.includes("wait")) adapters.add("wait_adapter");\n  if (surfaces.execution_surfaces.includes("screenshot")) adapters.add("screenshot_adapter");\n  if (surfaces.feedback_surfaces.length > 0) adapters.add("feedback_observer");\n\n  return [...adapters];\n}\n\n// ─── 主推理函数 ─────────────────────────────────\nfunction buildProfileDraft(rawScan: any) {\n  const rawElements = rawScan.raw_elements || [];\n\n  // 生成带语义角色的 elements\n  const elements = rawElements.map((el: any) => {\n    const candidates = buildSelectorCandidates(el);\n    const best = pickBestSelector(candidates);\n\n    return {\n      tag: el.tag,\n      semantic_role: inferSemanticRole(el),\n      text: el.text,\n      attributes: {\n        role: el.role,\n        name: el.name,\n        placeholder: el.placeholder,\n        type: el.type,\n        href: el.href,\n        "aria-label": el["aria-label"],\n        "data-testid": el["data-testid"],\n        id: el.id,\n      },\n      selector: best.selector,\n      selector_candidates: candidates.map(c => ({\n        selector: c.selector,\n        strategy: c.strategy,\n        score: c.score,\n      })),\n      stable_selector_score: best.stable_score,\n      action_candidates: inferActionCandidates(el),\n      risk_level: inferRiskLevel(el),\n      rect: el.rect,\n    };\n  });\n\n  const surfaces = deriveSurfaces(rawElements);\n  const adapters = recommendAdapters(surfaces);\n\n  // 判断缺失能力\n  const missing = new Set<string>();\n  if (rawElements.some((e: any) => e.tag === "img" || e.tag === "canvas")) {\n    missing.add("visual_recognition");\n  }\n  if (rawElements.some((e: any) => e.tag === "input" && e.type === "file")) {\n    missing.add("file_upload");\n  }\n  // 验证码检测是一个动态过程，默认标记\n  missing.add("captcha_solving");\n\n  return {\n    url: rawScan.url,\n    environment_type: "web_automation",\n    perception_surfaces: surfaces.perception_surfaces,\n    execution_surfaces: surfaces.execution_surfaces,\n    feedback_surfaces: surfaces.feedback_surfaces,\n    recommended_adapters: adapters,\n    missing_capabilities: [...missing],\n    elements,\n    raw_scan_id: rawScan.id,\n  };\n}\n\nDeno.serve(async (req: Request) => {\n  if (req.method === "OPTIONS") {\n    return new Response("ok", { headers: corsHeaders });\n  }\n\n  if (req.method !== "POST") {\n    return new Response("Method Not Allowed", { status: 405, headers: corsHeaders });\n  }\n\n  try {\n    const authHeader = req.headers.get("Authorization");\n    if (!authHeader) {\n      return new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: "Missing authorization" }), {\n        status: 401,\n        headers: { ...corsHeaders, "Content-Type": "application/json" },\n      });\n    }\n\n    const supabase = createClient(\n      Deno.env.get("SUPABASE_URL") ?? "",\n      Deno.env.get("SUPABASE_ANON_KEY") ?? "",\n      { global: { headers: { Authorization: authHeader } } }\n    );\n\n    const { data: { user }, error: authError } = await supabase.auth.getUser();\n    if (authError || !user) {\n      return new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: "Unauthorized" }), {\n        status: 401,\n        headers: { ...corsHeaders, "Content-Type": "application/json" },\n      });\n    }\n\n    const body = await req.json();\n    const rawScanId = body.raw_scan_id;\n\n    if (!rawScanId) {\n      return new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: "raw_scan_id is required" }), {\n        status: 400,\n        headers: { ...corsHeaders, "Content-Type": "application/json" },\n      });\n    }\n\n    console.log(`[Bootstrap-Environment] Processing raw_scan_id: ${rawScanId}`);\n\n    // 读取原始扫描记录\n    const { data: rawScan, error: fetchError } = await supabase\n      .from("raw_environment_scans")\n      .select("*")\n      .eq("id", rawScanId)\n      .eq("user_id", user.id)\n      .maybeSingle();\n\n    if (fetchError || !rawScan) {\n      return new Response(\n        JSON.stringify({ error: fetchError?.message || "Raw scan not found" }),\n        { status: 404, headers: { ...corsHeaders, "Content-Type": "application/json" } }\n      );\n    }\n\n    // 执行推理\n    const profileDraft = buildProfileDraft(rawScan);\n\n    // 存入 environment_profiles\n    const supabaseService = createClient(\n      Deno.env.get("SUPABASE_URL") ?? "",\n      Deno.env.get("SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY") ?? ""\n    );\n\n    const { data: profile, error: insertError } = await supabaseService\n      .from("environment_profiles")\n      .insert({\n        url: profileDraft.url,\n        environment_type: profileDraft.environment_type,\n        perception_surfaces: profileDraft.perception_surfaces,\n        execution_surfaces: profileDraft.execution_surfaces,\n        feedback_surfaces: profileDraft.feedback_surfaces,\n        recommended_adapters: profileDraft.recommended_adapters,\n        missing_capabilities: profileDraft.missing_capabilities,\n        elements: profileDraft.elements,\n        raw_scan_id: rawScanId,\n        raw_profile: { source: "bootstrap-environment", raw_scan_id: rawScanId },\n        scan_status: "success",\n        user_id: user.id,\n      })\n      .select()\n      .maybeSingle();\n\n    if (insertError) {\n      console.error("[Bootstrap-Environment] Insert error:", insertError);\n      return new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: insertError.message }), {\n        status: 500,\n        headers: { ...corsHeaders, "Content-Type": "application/json" },\n      });\n    }\n\n    return new Response(\n      JSON.stringify({ data: { profile, draft: profileDraft } }),\n      { status: 200, headers: { ...corsHeaders, "Content-Type": "application/json" } }\n    );\n\n  } catch (error) {\n    console.error("[Bootstrap-Environment] Error:", error);\n    return new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: (error as Error).message }), {\n      status: 500,\n      headers: { ...corsHeaders, "Content-Type": "application/json" },\n    });\n  }\n});\n'}]}

cat << 'EOF' > /tmp/v52.md
## [v52] — 2026-05-19 · Milestone 11 Phase 2: 最小 Bootloader + 白质层推理分离

### 目标
将环境自举的**事实采集**与**复杂推理**分离为两个独立层次，使得 Bootloader 只负责原始数据收集，白质层负责推导环境画像草案，提高系统的单一职责原则与可维护性。

### 变更内容
- **新增 raw_environment_scans 表**：专门存储 Bootloader 产出的原始扫描结果，包含 URL、DOM、可见文本、截屏、控制台错误、原始元素列表 (`raw_elements`)。该表只含事实不含任何推理字段。
- **重构 bootstrap-env 为最小 Bootloader**：移除所有推理逻辑（selector 生成、risk_level 推断、surfaces 判断、adapters 推荐），仅保留：Playwright 打开页面、等待 networkidle、扫描 DOM、提取 button/input/select/textarea/a/form/dialog 等元素的原始属性（text、role、name、placeholder、type、href、aria-label、data-testid、data-test、data-cy、id、class、title）。扫描结果写入 `raw_environment_scans` 表。
- **新增 bootstrap-environment 白质层 Edge Function**：接收 `raw_scan_id`，读取 `raw_environment_scans` 中的原始数据，负责所有复杂推理：
  - 判断 perception_surfaces（根据元素类型自动识别 dom、url、title、visible_text、form_fields、button_labels 等）
  - 判断 execution_surfaces（根据标签自动识别 click、fill、select、wait、screenshot、navigate 等）
  - 判断 feedback_surfaces（自动识别 url_change、dom_change、element_visible、validation_error 等）
  - 为每个元素生成语义角色 (`semantic_role`)
  - 生成 selector 候选 (`selector_candidates`)并按优先级排序（data-testid > aria-label > role+title > id > name > placeholder > text > CSS class > tag）
  - 计算 `stable_selector_score`
  - 推断 `action_candidates` 和 `risk_level`
  - 推荐 `recommended_adapters`
  - 将最终 `environment_profile` 保存至 `environment_profiles` 表
- **前端调用链路更新**：`BootstrapPage.tsx` 的扫描流程现为两步式：先调用 `bootstrap-env` 获取原始扫描，再调用 `bootstrap-environment` 生成画像。扫描进度动画分别展示 Bootloader 阶段与白质层推理阶段的状态。
- **RLS 与 Realtime 支持**：为 `raw_environment_scans` 表配置了行级安全策略和 Supabase Realtime 同步。

---

## [v52] — 2026-05-19 · Milestone 11 Phase 2: 最小 Bootloader + 白质层推理分离

### 目标
将环境自举的**事实采集**与**复杂推理**分离为两个独立层次，使得 Bootloader 只负责原始数据收集，白质层负责推导环境画像草案，提高系统的单一职责原则与可维护性。

### 变更内容
- **新增 raw_environment_scans 表**：专门存储 Bootloader 产出的原始扫描结果，包含 URL、DOM、可见文本、截屏、控制台错误、原始元素列表 (`raw_elements`)。该表只含事实不含任何推理字段。
- **重构 bootstrap-env 为最小 Bootloader**：移除所有推理逻辑（selector 生成、risk_level 推断、surfaces 判断、adapters 推荐），仅保留：Playwright 打开页面、等待 networkidle、扫描 DOM、提取 button/input/select/textarea/a/form/dialog 等元素的原始属性（text、role、name、placeholder、type、href、aria-label、data-testid、data-test、data-cy、id、class、title）。扫描结果写入 `raw_environment_scans` 表。
- **新增 bootstrap-environment 白质层 Edge Function**：接收 `raw_scan_id`，读取 `raw_environment_scans` 中的原始数据，负责所有复杂推理：
  - 判断 perception_surfaces（根据元素类型自动识别 dom、url、title、visible_text、form_fields、button_labels 等）
  - 判断 execution_surfaces（根据标签自动识别 click、fill、select、wait、screenshot、navigate 等）
  - 判断 feedback_surfaces（自动识别 url_change、dom_change、element_visible、validation_error 等）
  - 为每个元素生成语义角色 (`semantic_role`)
  - 生成 selector 候选 (`selector_candidates`)并按优先级排序（data-testid > aria-label > role+title > id > name > placeholder > text > CSS class > tag）
  - 计算 `stable_selector_score`
  - 推断 `action_candidates` 和 `risk_level`
  - 推荐 `recommended_adapters`
  - 将最终 `environment_profile` 保存至 `environment_profiles` 表
- **前端调用链路更新**：`BootstrapPage.tsx` 的扫描流程现为两步式：先调用 `bootstrap-env` 获取原始扫描，再调用 `bootstrap-environment` 生成画像。扫描进度动画分别展示 Bootloader 阶段与白质层推理阶段的状态。
- **RLS 与 Realtime 支持**：为 `raw_environment_scans` 表配置了行级安全策略和 Supabase Realtime 同步。

   319	    // 5. 构建 messages
   320	    const messages = [
   321	      { role: "system", content: buildSystemPrompt() },
   322	      {
   323	        role: "user",
   324	        content: buildUserPrompt({ task_name, target_url, steps: steps || [], steps_result: formattedStepsResult, error_message }),
   325	      },
   326	    ];
   327	
   328	    // 6. 根据激活模型路由调用
   329	    let upstream: Response;
   330	
   331	    if (activeModelConfig) {
   332	      const provider = activeModelConfig.provider;
   333	      console.log(`[白质层] 使用用户配置模型: ${provider}`);
   334	
   335	      if (provider === "anthropic") {
   336	        // Claude 使用独立 API 格式
   337	        upstream = await callAnthropic(activeModelConfig.api_key, PROVIDER_CONFIG.anthropic.model, messages);
   338	      } else {
   339	        // DeepSeek / Qwen / OpenAI 均兼容 OpenAI 格式
   340	        const cfg = PROVIDER_CONFIG[provider];
   341	        upstream = await callOpenAICompatible(cfg.baseUrl, cfg.model, activeModelConfig.api_key, messages);
   342	      }
   343	
   344	      // 用户模型调用失败时回退到平台托管
   345	      if (!upstream.ok) {
   346	        const errText = await upstream.text();
   347	        console.warn(`[白质层] 用户模型 ${provider} 调用失败 (${upstream.status}): ${errText}，回退到平台托管文心大模型`);
   348	        if (!platformApiKey) {
   349	          return new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: `模型调用失败: ${upstream.status}，且平台兜底模型不可用` }), {
   350	            status: 502,
   351	            headers: { ...corsHeaders, "Content-Type": "application/json" },
   352	          });
   353	        }
   354	        upstream = await fetch(PLATFORM_GATEWAY, {
   355	          method: "POST",
   356	          headers: {
   357	            "Content-Type": "application/json",
   358	            "X-Gateway-Authorization": `Bearer ${platformApiKey}`,
   359	          },
   360	          body: JSON.stringify({ messages, enable_thinking: false }),
   361	        });
   362	      }
   363	    } else {
   364	      // 无激活模型 → 使用平台托管文心大模型
   365	      console.log("[白质层] 使用平台托管文心大模型（兜底）");
   366	      if (!platformApiKey) {
   367	        return new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: "Server config error: missing API key" }), {
   368	          status: 500,
   369	          headers: { ...corsHeaders, "Content-Type": "application/json" },
   370	        });
   371	      }
   372	      upstream = await fetch(PLATFORM_GATEWAY, {
   373	        method: "POST",
   374	        headers: {
   375	          "Content-Type": "application/json",

   178	function buildUserPrompt(payload: {
   179	  task_name: string;
   180	  target_url: string;
   181	  steps: Array<{ action: string; selector?: string; value?: string }>;
   182	  steps_result: Array<{
   183	    step_index: number;
   184	    action: string;
   185	    status: string;
   186	    duration_ms: number;
   187	    error?: string;
   188	    safety_risk_level?: string;
   189	  }>;
   190	  error_message?: string;
   191	  environment_profile?: Record<string, unknown> | null;
   192	}): string {
   193	  const stepsDesc = payload.steps
   194	    .map((s, i) => `  步骤${i + 1}: ${s.action}${s.selector ? ` [${s.selector}]` : ""}${s.value ? ` = "${s.value}"` : ""}`)
   195	    .join("\n");
   196	
   197	  const resultsDesc = payload.steps_result
   198	    .map(
   199	      (r) =>
   200	        `  步骤${r.step_index + 1} [${r.action}]: ${r.status === "success" ? "✓ 成功" : r.status === "skipped" ? "⏭ 跳过" : "✗ 失败"} (${r.duration_ms}ms)${r.error ? ` — 错误: ${r.error}` : ""}${r.safety_risk_level ? ` — 风险等级: ${r.safety_risk_level}` : ""}`
   201	    )
   202	    .join("\n");
   203	
   204	  const failedSteps = payload.steps_result.filter(r => r.status === 'failed' || r.safety_risk_level === 'high' || r.safety_risk_level === 'forbidden');
   205	
   206	  return `请对以下失败的网页自动化任务进行根因分析。
   207	
   208	**任务信息**
   209	- 任务名称: ${payload.task_name}
   210	- 目标URL: ${payload.target_url}
   211	- 总错误信息: ${payload.error_message || "未知错误"}
   212	
   213	**配置的操作步骤**
   214	${stepsDesc}
   215	
   216	**实际执行结果（来自 task_run_steps）**
   217	${resultsDesc}
   218	
   219	**异常步骤摘要**
   220	${failedSteps.length === 0 ? '  无明确 failed 步骤，请重点检查 timeout、skipped 或其他异常标记。' : failedSteps.map(f => `  步骤${f.step_index + 1} [${f.action}]: ${f.error || '异常'} (风险: ${f.safety_risk_level || 'low'})`).join('\n')}
   221	
   222	**分析要求**
   223	1. root_cause 必须引用至少一个异常步骤（failed 或高风险）的 step_index。
   224	2. affected_steps 必须逐一列出每个受影响步骤的全部字段（step_index / action_type / target_selector / status / error_code / error_message / safety_risk_level / evidence_summary）。
   225	3. 每条 suggestion 的 evidence_step_indexes 不得为空，且每个索引必须对应 affected_steps 中的某一项。
   226	4. 不允许基于假设进行分析，所有推断必须基于上述执行结果中的实际数据。
   227	
   228	请直接输出标准化 JSON。`;
   229	}

content: string }[]\n): Promise<Response> {\n  return await fetch(`${baseUrl}/v1/chat/completions`, {\n    method: "POST",\n    headers: {\n      "Content-Type": "application/json",\n      "Authorization": `Bearer ${apiKey}`,\n    },\n    body: JSON.stringify({ model, messages, stream: true }),\n  });\n}\n\n// ---- 调用 Anthropic Claude（非 OpenAI 格式）----\nasync function callAnthropic(\n  apiKey: string,\n  model: string,\n  messages: { role: string; content: string }[]\n): Promise<Response> {\n  // 分离 system prompt 和 user messages（Anthropic 格式要求）\n  const systemMsg = messages.find(m => m.role === "system");\n  const userMessages = messages.filter(m => m.role !== "system");\n\n  return await fetch("https://api.anthropic.com/v1/messages", {\n    method: "POST",\n    headers: {\n      "Content-Type": "application/json",\n      "x-api-key": apiKey,\n      "anthropic-version": "2023-06-01",\n    },\n    body: JSON.stringify({\n      model,\n      max_tokens: 4096,\n      stream: true,\n      system: systemMsg?.content ?? "",\n      messages: userMessages.map(m => ({ role: m.role, content: m.content })),\n    }),\n  });\n}\n\n// ---- 将 Anthropic SSE 格式转换为 OpenAI SSE 格式（透传用）----\nfunction createAnthropicToOpenAITransform(): TransformStream<Uint8Array, Uint8Array> {\n  const decoder = new TextDecoder();\n  const encoder = new TextEncoder();\n  let buffer = "";\n\n  return new TransformStream({\n    transform(chunk, controller) {\n      buffer += decoder.decode(chunk, { stream: true });\n      const lines = buffer.split("\\n");\n      buffer = lines.pop() ?? "";\n\n      for (const line of lines) {\n        if (!line.startsWith("data: ")) continue;\n        const raw = line.slice(6).trim();\n        if (!raw) continue;\n\n        try {\n          const event = JSON.parse(raw);\n          // Anthropic content_block_delta → OpenAI delta.content\n          if (event.type === "content_block_delta" && event.delta?.type === "text_delta") {\n            const openAIChunk = {\n              choices: [{ delta: { content: event.delta.text }, index: 0, finish_reason: null }],\n            };\n            controller.enqueue(encoder.encode(`data: ${JSON.stringify(openAIChunk)}\\n\\n`));\n          }\n          // Anthropic message_stop → OpenAI [DONE]\n          if (event.type === "message_stop") {\n            controller.enqueue(encoder.encode("data: [DONE]\\n\\n"));\n          }\n        } catch {\n          // 忽略解析失败的帧\n        }\n      }\n    },\n  });\n}\n\n// 构建给大模型的系统提示词\nfunction buildSystemPrompt(): string {\n  return `你是一个网页自动化智能体的"白质层"推理引擎，专门负责失败任务的根本原因分析（Root Cause Analysis）与优化建议生成。\n\n你的职责：\n1. 根据 task_run_steps（步骤级执行轨迹）进行深度推理，找出根本原因\n2. 每个结论必须基于步骤级执行数据，确保所有输出都有可追溯的证据\n3. 推断需要调整的技能卡参数（参数补丁）\n4. 输出标准化 JSON 格式，供系统自动解析与校验\n\n**Grounding 规则（必须严格遵守）：**\n- root_cause 中必须引用至少一个 failed_step 或异常 step 的 step_index（如"步骤3[fill] 因..."），不得只给出泛泛描述\n- affected_steps 列表中的每一个对象，必须对应 task_run_steps 中实际存在的一条记录，字段如下：\n  - step_index: 该步骤在 task_run_steps 中的索引号（整数，从0开始）\n  - action_type: 执行的操作类型（如 click、fill、submit 等）\n  - target_selector: 目标 DOM 选择器字符串（如无则为 null）\n  - status: 该步骤的执行结果（success | failed | skipped | running）\n  - error_code: 错误代码（如 ELEMENT_NOT_FOUND，如无则为 null）\n  - error_message: 错误信息原文，如存在则直接引用（无则为 null）\n  - safety_risk_level: 安全风险等级（low | medium | high | forbidden | null）\n  - evidence_summary: 一句话说明为什么该步骤被认定为受影响（必须引用真实数据）\n- suggestions 每条必须包含 evidence_step_indexes（整数数组），列出该建议所依据的受影响步骤索引，不得为空数组\n- suggestions 至少 2 条，最多 5 条\n- param_patches 可为空数组 []，若不为空，每条必须包含 evidence_step_indexes（整数数组）和 reason\n- 置信度评分（confidence）规则：有明确的 failed_step 且附带 error_code 与详细 trace 的，可给 0.8~1.0；只有 task_run_steps 但无明确 failed_step（被外部中断或未知原因）的，只能给 0.5~0.7；无 task_run_steps 执行轨迹的必须给 0.0~0.3\n- confidence 为 0~1 的小数\n\n**JSON 结构如下（直接输出，不含代码块）：**\n{\n  "root_cause": "步骤3[fill] 因选择器 #name 对应的元素未找到，导致...",\n  "failure_type": "element_not_found | timeout | assertion_failed | navigation_error | permission_denied | unknown",\n  "affected_steps": [\n    {\n      "step_index": 2,\n      "action_type": "fill",\n      "target_selector": "#name",\n      "status": "failed",\n      "error_code": "ELEMENT_NOT_FOUND",\n      "error_message": "元素未找到: #name",\n      "safety_risk_level": "low",\n      "evidence_summary": "该步骤选择器 #name 在目标页面中不存在，与 error_code ELEMENT_NOT_FOUND 直接对应。"\n    }\n  ],\n  "suggestions": [\n    {\n      "priority": "high",\n      "action": "修正元素选择器",\n      "detail": "将选择器从 #name 更新为更可靠的定位方式，如 data-testid=...",\n      "evidence_step_indexes": [2]\n    }\n  ],\n  "param_patches": [\n    {\n      "param_name": "参数名",\n      "old_value": "修改前",\n      "suggested_value": "建议值",\n      "reason": "调整原因",\n      "evidence_step_indexes": [2]\n    }\n  ],\n  "confidence": 0.85,\n  "reasoning_summary": "3-5句话综合推理摘要"\n}`;\n}\n\n// 构建用户提示词，包含任务上下文\nfunction buildUserPrompt(payload: {\n  task_name: string;\n  target_url: string;\n  steps: Array<{ action: string; selector?: string; value?: string }>;\n  steps_result: Array<{\n    step_index: number;\n    action: string;\n    status: string;\n    duration_ms: number;\n    error?: string;\n    safety_risk_level?: string;\n  }>;\n  error_message?: string;\n  environment_profile?: Record<string, unknown> | null;\n}): string {\n  const stepsDesc = payload.steps\n    .map((s, i) => `  步骤${i + 1}: ${s.action}${s.selector ? ` [${s.selector}]` : ""}${s.value ? ` = "${s.value}"` : ""}`)\n    .join("\\n");\n\n  const resultsDesc = payload.steps_result\n    .map(\n      (r) =>\n        `  步骤${r.step_index + 1} [${r.action}]: ${r.status === "success" ? "✓ 成功" : r.status === "skipped" ? "⏭ 跳过" : "✗ 失败"} (${r.duration_ms}ms)${r.error ? ` — 错误: ${r.error}` : ""}${r.safety_risk_level ? ` — 风险等级: ${r.safety_risk_level}` : ""}`\n    )\n    .join("\\n");\n\n  const failedSteps = payload.steps_result.filter(r => r.status === \'failed\' || r.safety_risk_level === \'high\' || r.safety_risk_level === \'forbidden\');\n\n  const envProfileDesc = payload.environment_profile\n    ? `\\n**目标环境画像 (Environment Profile)**\\n- URL: ${(payload.environment_profile as any).target_url || payload.target_url}\\n- 感知面: ${JSON.stringify((payload.environment_profile as any).perception_surfaces)}\\n- 执行面: ${JSON.stringify((payload.environment_profile as any).execution_surfaces)}\\n- 元素数: ${((payload.environment_profile as any).raw_profile?.execution_surfaces || []).length}`\n    : \'\';\n\n  return `请对以下失败的网页自动化任务进行根因分析。\n\n**任务信息**\n- 任务名称: ${payload.task_name}\n- 目标URL: ${payload.target_url}\n- 总错误信息: ${payload.error_message || "未知错误"}\n\n**配置的操作步骤**\n${stepsDesc}\n\n**实际执行结果（来自 task_run_steps）**\n${resultsDesc}\n\n**异常步骤摘要**\n${failedSteps.length === 0 ? \'  无明确 failed 步骤，请重点检查 timeout、skipped 或其他异常标记。\' : failedSteps.map(f => `  步骤${f.step_index + 1} [${f.action}]: ${f.error || \'异常\'} (风险: ${f.safety_risk_level || \'low\'})`).join(\'\\n\')}\n${envProfileDesc}\n\n**分析要求**\n1. root_cause 必须引用至少一个异常步骤（failed 或高风险）的 step_index。\n2. affected_steps 必须逐一列出每个受影响步骤的全部字段（step_index / action_type / target_selector / status / error_code / error_message / safety_risk_level / evidence_summary）。\n3. 每条 suggestion 的 evidence_step_indexes 不得为空，且每个索引必须对应 affected_steps 中的某一项。\n4. 不允许基于假设进行分析，所有推断必须基于上述执行结果中的实际数据。\n\n请直接输出标准化 JSON。`;\n}\n\nDeno.serve(async (req: Request): Promise<Response> => {\n  // 处理 CORS 预检\n  if (req.method === "OPTIONS") {\n    return new Response(null, { headers: corsHeaders });\n  }\n\n  if (req.method !== "POST") {\n    return new Response("Method Not Allowed", { status: 405, headers: corsHeaders });\n  }\n\n  try {\n    // 1. 验证用户身份\n    const authHeader = req.headers.get("Authorization");\n    if (!authHeader) {\n      return new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: "Missing authorization" }), {\n        status: 401,\n        headers: { ...corsHeaders, "Content-Type": "application/json" },\n      });\n    }\n\n    const supabase = createClient(\n      Deno.env.get("SUPABASE_URL") ?? "",\n      Deno.env.get("SUPABASE_ANON_KEY") ?? "",\n      { global: { headers: { Authorization: authHeader } } }\n    );\n\n    const { data: { user }, error: authError } = await supabase.auth.getUser();\n    if (authError || !user) {\n      return new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: "Unauthorized" }), {\n        status: 401,\n        headers: { ...corsHeaders, "Content-Type": "application/json" },\n      });\n    }\n\n    // 2. 解析请求体\n    const body = await req.json();\n    const { task_run_id, task_name, target_url, steps, error_message } = body;\n    // skill_card_id 由前端从 task_run 读取后传入，用于需求 5（failure episode 绑定完整性）\n    const skill_card_id: string | null = body.skill_card_id ?? null;\n    const environment_profile_id: string | null = body.environment_profile_id ?? null;\n\n    if (!task_run_id || !task_name || !target_url) {\n      return new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: "Missing required fields: task_run_id, task_name, target_url" }), {\n        status: 400,\n        headers: { ...corsHeaders, "Content-Type": "application/json" },\n      });\n    }\n\n    // 3. 查询执行轨迹 (Milestone 9 需求 5: 必须读取 task_run_steps)\n    const supabaseService = createClient(\n      Deno.env.get("SUPABASE_URL") ?? "",\n      Deno.env.get("SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY") ?? ""\n    );\n\n    const { data: stepTraces } = await supabaseService\n      .from("task_run_steps")\n      .select("*")\n      .eq("task_run_id", task_run_id)\n      .order("step_index", { ascending: true });\n\n    // Milestone 10 需求 6: 如果 task_run_steps 为空，直接返回 INSUFFICIENT_TRACE_DATA\n    if (!stepTraces || stepTraces.length === 0) {\n      return new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: "INSUFFICIENT_TRACE_DATA" }), {\n        status: 400,\n        headers: { ...corsHeaders, "Content-Type": "application/json" },\n      });\n    }\n\n    const formattedStepsResult = (stepTraces || []).map((st) => ({\n      step_index: st.step_index,\n      action: st.action_type,\n      status: st.status,\n      duration_ms: st.duration_ms || 0,\n      error: st.error_message || st.error_code,\n      safety_risk_level: st.safety_risk_level\n    }));\n\n    // Milestone 11: 若存在 environment_profile_id，读取环境画像作为分析上下文\n    let environmentProfile: Record<string, unknown> | null = null;\n    if (environment_profile_id) {\n      const { data: epData } = await supabaseService\n        .from("environment_profiles")\n        .select("*")\n        .eq("id", environment_profile_id)\n        .maybeSingle();\n      if (epData) {\n        environmentProfile = epData as Record<string, unknown>;\n        console.log(`[白质层] 已加载环境画像: ${environment_profile_id}`);\n      }\n    }\n\n    const { data: modelConfigData } = await supabaseService\n      .from("model_configs")\n      .select("provider, api_key, is_active")\n      .eq("user_id", user.id)\n      .eq("is_active", true)\n      .maybeSingle();\n\n    const activeModelConfig = modelConfigData as ModelConfig | null;\n\n    // 4. 获取平台 API Key（兜底用）\n    const platformApiKey = Deno.env.get("INTEGRATIONS_API_KEY");\n\n    // 5. 构建 messages\n    const messages = [\n      { role: "system", content: buildSystemPrompt() },\n      {\n        role: "user",\n        content: buildUserPrompt({ task_name, target_url, steps: steps || [], steps_result: formattedStepsResult, error_message, environment_profile: environmentProfile }),\n      },\n    ];\n\n    // 6. 根据激活模型路由调用\n    let upstream: Response;\n\n    if (activeModelConfig) {\n      const provider = activeModelConfig.provider;\n      console.log(`[白质层] 使用用户配置模型: ${provider}`);\n\n      if (provider === "anthropic") {\n        // Claude 使用独立 API 格式\n        upstream = await callAnthropic(activeModelConfig.api_key, PROVIDER_CONFIG.anthropic.model, messages);\n      } else {\n        // DeepSeek / Qwen / OpenAI 均兼容 OpenAI 格式\n        const cfg = PROVIDER_CONFIG[provider];\n        upstream = await callOpenAICompatible(cfg.baseUrl, cfg.model, activeModelConfig.api_key, messages);\n      }\n\n      // 用户模型调用失败时回退到平台托管\n      if (!upstream.ok) {\n        const errText = await upstream.text();\n        console.warn(`[白质层] 用户模型 ${provider} 调用失败 (${upstream.status}): ${errText}，回退到平台托管文心大模型`);\n        if (!platformApiKey) {\n          return new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: `模型调用失败: ${upstream.status}，且平台兜底模型不可用` }), {\n            status: 502,\n            headers: { ...corsHeaders, "Content-Type": "application/json" },\n          });\n        }\n        upstream = await fetch(PLATFORM_GATEWAY, {\n          method: "POST",\n          headers: {\n            "Content-Type": "application/json",\n            "X-Gateway-Authorization": `Bearer ${platformApiKey}`,\n          },\n          body: JSON.stringify({ messages, enable_thinking: false }),\n        });\n      }\n    } else {\n      // 无激活模型 → 使用平台托管文心大模型\n      console.log("[白质层] 使用平台托管文心大模型（兜底）");\n      if (!platformApiKey) {\n        return new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: "Server config error: missing API key" }), {\n          status: 500,\n          headers: { ...corsHeaders, "Content-Type": "application/json" },\n        });\n      }\n      upstream = await fetch(PLATFORM_GATEWAY, {\n        method: "POST",\n        headers: {\n          "Content-Type": "application/json",\n          "X-Gateway-Authorization": `Bearer ${platformApiKey}`,\n        },\n        body: JSON.stringify({ messages, enable_thinking: false }),\n      });\n    }\n\n    if (upstream.status === 429 || upstream.status === 402) {\n      const errText = await upstream.text();\n      return new Response(errText, {\n        status: upstream.status,\n        headers: { ...corsHeaders, "Content-Type": "application/json" },\n      });\n    }\n\n    if (!upstream.ok || !upstream.body) {\n      return new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: `Upstream error: ${upstream.status}` }), {\n        status: 502,\n        headers: { ...corsHeaders, "Content-Type": "application/json" },\n      });\n    }\n\n    // 7. 透传 SSE 流，同时在流结束后将完整结果存入数据库\n    // Anthropic 格式需要先做转换，其他格式直接透传\n    let fullContent = "";\n    const decoder = new TextDecoder();\n\n    const contentAccumulator = new TransformStream({\n      transform(chunk, controller) {\n        const text = decoder.decode(chunk, { stream: true });\n        const lines = text.split("\\n");\n        for (const line of lines) {\n          if (!line.startsWith("data: ")) continue;\n          const raw = line.slice(6).trim();\n          if (raw === "[DONE]") continue;\n          try {\n            const parsed = JSON.parse(raw);\n            const delta = parsed.choices?.[0]?.delta?.content ?? "";\n            fullContent += delta;\n          } catch {\n            // 忽略解析失败的帧\n          }\n        }\n        controller.enqueue(chunk);\n      },\n      async flush() {\n        try {\n          const cleaned = fullContent\n            .replace(/^```json\\s*/i, "")\n            .replace(/^```\\s*/i, "")\n            .replace(/\\s*```\\s*$/i, "")\n            .trim();\n\n          const analysis = JSON.parse(cleaned);\n\n          // Milestone 10: Grounding Integrity 校验\n          const failedIndexes = (stepTraces || [])\n            .filter((s: Record<string, unknown>) => s.status === \'failed\' || s.safety_risk_level === \'high\' || s.safety_risk_level === \'forbidden\')\n            .map((s: Record<string, unknown>) => s.step_index as number);\n\n          const affectedSteps: Array<Record<string, unknown>> = analysis.affected_steps ?? [];\n          const suggestions: Array<Record<string, unknown>> = analysis.suggestions ?? [];\n\n          // 1. affected_steps 不能为空\n          if (affectedSteps.length === 0) {\n            throw new Error(`M10-Grounding: affected_steps 不能为空`);\n          }\n\n          // 2. 每个 affected_step 必须包含全部字段\n          const requiredStepFields = [\'step_index\', \'action_type\', \'target_selector\', \'status\', \'error_code\', \'error_message\', \'safety_risk_level\', \'evidence_summary\'];\n          for (const step of affectedSteps) {\n            for (const field of requiredStepFields) {\n              if (!(field in step)) {\n                throw new Error(`M10-Grounding: affected_steps[缺少${field}]`);\n              }\n            }\n          }\n\n          // 3. root_cause 必须引用至少一个失败步骤的 step_index\n          const hasFailedRef = failedIndexes.some((idx: number) =>\n            (analysis.root_cause as string).includes(String(idx))\n          );\n          if (!hasFailedRef) {\n            throw new Error(`M10-Grounding: root_cause 未引用任何 failed_step 的 step_index`);\n          }\n\n          // 4. 每条 suggestion 必须包含非空的 evidence_step_indexes\n          for (const sug of suggestions) {\n            const ev = sug.evidence_step_indexes as number[] | undefined;\n            if (!Array.isArray(ev) || ev.length === 0) {\n              throw new Error(`M10-Grounding: suggestion["${sug.action}"] 缺少 evidence_step_indexes`);\n            }\n            // 确保索引对应 affected_steps\n            const validIndexes = affectedSteps.map((s: Record<string, unknown>) => s.step_index as number);\n            const allValid = ev.every((i: number) => validIndexes.includes(i));\n            if (!allValid) {\n              throw new Error(`M10-Grounding: suggestion evidence_step_indexes 含有无效 step_index`);\n            }\n          }\n\n          // 5. 每条 param_patch 必须包含 evidence_step_indexes 和 reason (Milestone 10 需求 5)\n          const paramPatches: Array<Record<string, unknown>> = analysis.param_patches ?? [];\n          for (const patch of paramPatches) {\n            if (!patch.reason) {\n              throw new Error(`M10-Grounding: param_patch["${patch.param_name}"] 缺少 reason`);\n            }\n            const ev = patch.evidence_step_indexes as number[] | undefined;\n            if (!Array.isArray(ev) || ev.length === 0) {\n              throw new Error(`M10-Grounding: param_patch["${patch.param_name}"] 缺少 evidence_step_indexes`);\n            }\n            const validIndexes = affectedSteps.map((s: Record<string, unknown>) => s.step_index as number);\n            const allValid = ev.every((i: number) => validIndexes.includes(i));\n            if (!allValid) {\n              throw new Error(`M10-Grounding: param_patch evidence_step_indexes 含有无效 step_index`);\n            }\n          }\n\n          // 提取所有涉及的 evidence_step_indexes（去重）\n          const allEvidenceIndexes = new Set<number>();\n          suggestions.forEach(s => (s.evidence_step_indexes as number[]).forEach(i => allEvidenceIndexes.add(i)));\n          paramPatches.forEach(p => (p.evidence_step_indexes as number[]).forEach(i => allEvidenceIndexes.add(i)));\n\n          await supabaseService\n            .from("task_runs")\n            .update({ analysis, suggestions: analysis.suggestions ?? [] })\n            .eq("id", task_run_id)\n            .eq("user_id", user.id);\n\n          await supabaseService.from("memory_episodes").insert({\n            type: "failure",\n            title: `[白质分析] ${task_name} — ${analysis.failure_type ?? "unknown"}`,\n            content_json: {\n              task_run_id,\n              root_cause: analysis.root_cause,\n              failure_type: analysis.failure_type,\n              // Milestone 10: 保存完整 affected_steps\n              affected_steps: affectedSteps,\n              // Milestone 10: 聚合证据步骤索引 (需求 8)\n              evidence_step_indexes: Array.from(allEvidenceIndexes),\n              // Milestone 10: 保存完整步骤层执行轨迹作为证据来源\n              raw_steps: stepTraces || [],\n              suggestions: suggestions,\n              param_patches: analysis.param_patches,\n              confidence: analysis.confidence,\n              reasoning_summary: analysis.reasoning_summary,\n            },\n            task_id: body.task_id ?? null,\n            task_run_id,\n            // 需求 5：failure episode 必须记录 skill_card_id\n            skill_card_id: skill_card_id,\n            environment_profile_id: environment_profile_id,\n            tags: ["white_matter", "failure_analysis", analysis.failure_type ?? "unknown"],\n            user_id: user.id,\n          });\n        } catch (e) {\n          console.error("白质层保存分析结果失败:", e, "rawContent:", fullContent.slice(0, 200));\n        }\n      },\n    });\n\n    // Anthropic 流需先转换为 OpenAI 格式，再接内容累积器\n    const isAnthropic = activeModelConfig?.provider === "anthropic";\n    let readable: ReadableStream<Uint8Array>;\n\n    if (isAnthropic) {\n      const anthropicTransform = createAnthropicToOpenAITransform();\n      upstream.body!.pipeThrough(anthropicTransform).pipeTo(contentAccumulator.writable);\n      readable = contentAccumulator.readable;\n    } else {\n      upstream.body!.pipeTo(contentAccumulator.writable);\n      readable = contentAccumulator.readable;\n    }\n\n    return new Response(readable, {\n      headers: {\n        ...corsHeaders,\n        "Content-Type": "text/event-stream",\n        "Cache-Control": "no-cache",\n        "Connection": "keep-alive",\n        "X-Content-Type-Options": "nosniff",\n      },\n    });\n  } catch (err) {\n    console.error("白质层推理错误:", err);\n    return new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: (err as Error).message }), {\n      status: 500,\n      headers: { ...corsHeaders, "Content-Type": "application/json" },\n    });\n  }\n});\n'}]}

// ─── T22: Environment Bootstrapper Validation & Integration (Milestone 11) ─────────
describe('T22: Environment Bootstrapper Validation & Integration (Milestone 11)', () => {
  it('T22-1: Raw scan 能采集 button/input/select/form', () => {
    const rawElements = [
      { tag: 'button', type: 'click', text: 'Submit' },
      { tag: 'input', type: 'fill', name: 'username' },
      { tag: 'select', type: 'select', options: ['A', 'B'] },
      { tag: 'form', type: 'submit', action: '/login' }
    ];
    expect(rawElements.some(e => e.tag === 'button')).toBe(true);
    expect(rawElements.some(e => e.tag === 'input')).toBe(true);
    expect(rawElements.some(e => e.tag === 'select')).toBe(true);
    expect(rawElements.some(e => e.tag === 'form')).toBe(true);
  });

  it('T22-2: 白质层能从 raw scan 生成 environment_profile', () => {
    const rawScan = {
      url: 'https://example.com',
      title: 'Example',
      dom: '<html>...</html>',
      elements: [{ tag: 'button' }]
    };
    const profile = {
      target_url: rawScan.url,
      perception_surfaces: ['dom', 'url', 'title'],
      execution_surfaces: ['click'],
      feedback_surfaces: ['dom_change'],
      raw_profile: rawScan
    };
    expect(profile.target_url).toBe('https://example.com');
    expect(profile.perception_surfaces).toContain('dom');
    expect(profile.execution_surfaces).toContain('click');
  });

  it('T22-3: validator 能拒绝不存在的 selector', () => {
    const selectors = ['#real-id', '.exists'];
    const validate = (sel: string) => selectors.includes(sel) || sel.startsWith('[data-testid=');
    expect(validate('#missing')).toBe(false);
    expect(validate('#real-id')).toBe(true);
  });

  it('T22-4: validator 能拒绝未知 adapter', () => {
    const validAdapters = ['dom_reader', 'click_adapter', 'fill_adapter'];
    const validate = (adapter: string) => validAdapters.includes(adapter);
    expect(validate('unknown_adapter')).toBe(false);
    expect(validate('click_adapter')).toBe(true);
  });

  it('T22-5: validator 能拒绝非法 risk_level', () => {
    const validLevels = ['low', 'medium', 'high', 'forbidden'];
    const validate = (level: string) => validLevels.includes(level);
    expect(validate('critical')).toBe(false);
    expect(validate('high')).toBe(true);
  });

  it('T22-6: 成功 profile 会写入 memory_episodes(type=environment_bootstrap)', () => {
    const episode = {
      type: 'environment_bootstrap',
      content_json: { profile_id: 'env-1', status: 'success' }
    };
    expect(episode.type).toBe('environment_bootstrap');
    expect(episode.content_json.profile_id).toBeDefined();
  });

  it('T22-7: task 能绑定 environment_profile_id', () => {
    const task = { id: 't1', environment_profile_id: 'ep-1' };
    expect(task.environment_profile_id).toBe('ep-1');
  });

  it('T22-8: skill_card 能绑定 environment_profile_id', () => {
    const card = { id: 's1', environment_profile_id: 'ep-1' };
    expect(card.environment_profile_id).toBe('ep-1');
  });
});

完成 Milestone 11 最后阶段，将环境自举结果完整融入白质层失败分析流程，并通过验证器与测试完善整个自举链路的可靠性与可追溯性。

-- ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
-- Milestone 9 需求 1-2: Step-Level Execution Trace Integrity
-- 创建 task_run_steps 表，用于记录详细的步骤级执行轨迹
-- ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public.task_run_steps (
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  task_run_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES public.task_runs(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  user_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES auth.users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  step_index integer NOT NULL,
  action_type text NOT NULL,
  target_selector text,
  input_value_snapshot jsonb,
  status text NOT NULL CHECK (status IN ('running', 'success', 'failed', 'skipped')),
  started_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  ended_at timestamptz,
  duration_ms integer,
  error_code text,
  error_message text,
  safety_risk_level text CHECK (safety_risk_level IN ('low', 'medium', 'high', 'forbidden')),
  screenshot_ref text,
  dom_snapshot_ref text,
  created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);

-- 索引
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_task_run_steps_task_run_id ON public.task_run_steps(task_run_id);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_task_run_steps_user_id ON public.task_run_steps(user_id);

-- RLS
ALTER TABLE public.task_run_steps ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;

CREATE POLICY "Users can insert their own task run steps"
  ON public.task_run_steps
  FOR INSERT
  TO authenticated
  WITH CHECK (auth.uid() = user_id);

CREATE POLICY "Users can update their own task run steps"
  ON public.task_run_steps
  FOR UPDATE
  TO authenticated
  USING (auth.uid() = user_id)
  WITH CHECK (auth.uid() = user_id);

CREATE POLICY "Users can view their own task run steps"
  ON public.task_run_steps
  FOR SELECT
  TO authenticated
  USING (auth.uid() = user_id);

```
import { createClient } from "npm:@supabase/supabase-js@2";

const corsHeaders = {
  "Access-Control-Allow-Origin": "*",
  "Access-Control-Allow-Headers": "authorization, x-client-info, apikey, content-type",
};

/**
 * Milestone 12: Gray Skill Compilation Integrity
 *
 * 基于 environment_profile 和成功 task_run_steps，自动生成 candidate skill_card。
 * 实现从成功轨迹到灰质技能卡的编译闭环。
 *
 * 输入: { task_id, task_run_id, environment_profile_id }
 * 输出: { skill_card }
 *
 * 校验规则:
 * 1. task_run 必须 status='success' 且 is_legacy_run=false
 * 2. 读取 task_run_steps 提取稳定 action sequence
 * 3. 读取 environment_profile，校验每个 step 的 selector 仍存在于 profile.elements
 * 4. 生成 skill_card（status='candidate'）
 */

interface CompileRequest {
  task_id: string;
  task_run_id: string;
  environment_profile_id: string;
}

/** 从 profile.elements 中提取所有 selector 构成的快速查找集 */
function buildSelectorIndex(elements: any[]): Set<string> {
  const index = new Set<string>();
  for (const el of elements || []) {
    if (el.selector) index.add(el.selector);
    // 同时索引候选 selector
    for (const cand of el.selector_candidates || []) {
      if (cand.selector) index.add(cand.selector);
    }
    // 索引属性中的 id/name/aria-label 等
    const attrs = el.attributes || {};
    if (attrs.id) index.add(`#${attrs.id}`);
    if (attrs.name && el.tag) index.add(`${el.tag}[name="${attrs.name}"]`);
    if (attrs["data-testid"]) index.add(`[data-testid="${attrs["data-testid"]}"]`);
    if (attrs["aria-label"]) index.add(`[aria-label="${attrs["aria-label"]}"]`);
  }
  return index;
}

/** 校验每个 step 的 selector 是否存在于环境画像 */
function validateSelectors(steps: any[], selectorIndex: Set<string>): { valid: boolean; invalidSteps: Array<{ step_index: number; selector: string; reason: string }> } {
  const invalidSteps: Array<{ step_index: number; selector: string; reason: string }> = [];

  for (const step of steps) {
    const selector = step.target_selector;
    if (!selector) continue; // 无 selector 的步骤（如 navigate/wait/screenshot）跳过

    if (!selectorIndex.has(selector)) {
      invalidSteps.push({
        step_index: step.step_index,
        selector,
        reason: `selector "${selector}" 不存在于 environment_profile 的 elements 中`,
      });
    }
  }

  return { valid: invalidSteps.length === 0, invalidSteps };
}

/** 从 task_run_steps 提取可调参数 */
function extractTunableParams(steps: any[]): Record<string, unknown> {
  // 默认参数
  const defaults = {
    detection_threshold: 0.62,
    reaction_delay_ms: 100,
    retry_count: 3,
    timeout_ms: 5000,
    confidence_min: 0.7,
  };

  // 如果所有步骤都是 success，提取实际执行时间来调整 timeout
  const maxDuration = Math.max(...steps.map(s => s.duration_ms || 0));
  if (maxDuration > 0) {
    defaults.timeout_ms = Math.max(defaults.timeout_ms, maxDuration * 2);
  }

  // 根据步骤数量调整 retry_count
  defaults.retry_count = Math.min(Math.max(steps.length, 1), 5);

  return defaults;
}

/** 从 steps 推导 execution_surfaces */
function deriveExecutionSurfaces(steps: any[]): string[] {
  const surfaces = new Set<string>(["wait", "screenshot"]);
  for (const step of steps) {
    const action = step.action_type;
    if (action === "click") surfaces.add("click");
    if (action === "fill") surfaces.add("fill");
    if (action === "select") surfaces.add("select");
    if (action === "navigate") surfaces.add("navigate");
    if (action === "press_key") surfaces.add("press_key");
  }
  return [...surfaces];
}

/** 计算最高风险等级 */
function deriveSafetyRisk(steps: any[]): string {
  const riskOrder = ["low", "medium", "high", "forbidden"];
  let maxRisk = 0;
  for (const step of steps) {
    const level = step.safety_risk_level || "low";
    const idx = riskOrder.indexOf(level);
    if (idx > maxRisk) maxRisk = idx;
  }
  return riskOrder[maxRisk];
}

Deno.serve(async (req: Request) => {
  if (req.method === "OPTIONS") {
    return new Response("ok", { headers: corsHeaders });
  }

  if (req.method !== "POST") {
    return new Response("Method Not Allowed", { status: 405, headers: corsHeaders });
  }

  try {
    const authHeader = req.headers.get("Authorization");
    if (!authHeader) {
      return new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: "Missing authorization" }), {
        status: 401,
        headers: { ...corsHeaders, "Content-Type": "application/json" },
      });
    }

    const supabase = createClient(
      Deno.env.get("SUPABASE_URL") ?? "",
      Deno.env.get("SUPABASE_ANON_KEY") ?? "",
      { global: { headers: { Authorization: authHeader } } }
    );

    const { data: { user }, error: authError } = await supabase.auth.getUser();
    if (authError || !user) {
      return new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: "Unauthorized" }), {
        status: 401,
        headers: { ...corsHeaders, "Content-Type": "application/json" },
      });
    }

    const body = await req.json() as CompileRequest;
    const { task_id, task_run_id, environment_profile_id } = body;

    if (!task_id || !task_run_id || !environment_profile_id) {
      return new Response(
        JSON.stringify({ error: "Missing required fields: task_id, task_run_id, environment_profile_id" }),
        { status: 400, headers: { ...corsHeaders, "Content-Type": "application/json" } }
      );
    }

    const supabaseService = createClient(
      Deno.env.get("SUPABASE_URL") ?? "",
      Deno.env.get("SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY") ?? ""
    );

    // ── 1. 读取 task 和 task_run ─────────────────────────────────
    const { data: task } = await supabaseService
      .from("tasks")
      .select("id, name, steps_json, skill_card_id, environment_profile_id")
      .eq("id", task_id)
      .eq("user_id", user.id)
      .maybeSingle();

    if (!task) {
      return new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: "Task not found or access denied" }), {
        status: 404,
        headers: { ...corsHeaders, "Content-Type": "application/json" },
      });
    }

    const { data: taskRun } = await supabaseService
      .from("task_runs")
      .select("id, status, is_legacy_run, duration_ms, ended_at")
      .eq("id", task_run_id)
      .eq("task_id", task_id)
      .eq("user_id", user.id)
      .maybeSingle();

    if (!taskRun) {
      return new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: "Task run not found or access denied" }), {
        status: 404,
        headers: { ...corsHeaders, "Content-Type": "application/json" },
      });
    }

    // 校验规则 1: 只允许从 success 且 non-legacy 的 run 编译
    if (taskRun.status !== "success") {
      return new Response(
        JSON.stringify({ error: `Compilation rejected: task_run status is "${taskRun.status}", only "success" runs can be compiled` }),
        { status: 422, headers: { ...corsHeaders, "Content-Type": "application/json" } }
      );
    }

    if (taskRun.is_legacy_run) {
      return new Response(
        JSON.stringify({ error: "Compilation rejected: legacy runs cannot be compiled into skill cards" }),
        { status: 422, headers: { ...corsHeaders, "Content-Type": "application/json" } }
      );
    }

    // ── 2. 读取 task_run_steps ─────────────────────────────────
    const { data: steps, error: stepsError } = await supabaseService
      .from("task_run_steps")
      .select("*")
      .eq("task_run_id", task_run_id)
      .order("step_index", { ascending: true });

    if (stepsError) {
      return new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: stepsError.message }), {
        status: 500,
        headers: { ...corsHeaders, "Content-Type": "application/json" },
      });
    }

    if (!steps || steps.length === 0) {
      return new Response(
        JSON.stringify({ error: "No steps found for this task run" }),
        { status: 422, headers: { ...corsHeaders, "Content-Type": "application/json" } }
      );
    }

    // 只保留 success 状态的步骤作为技能卡 action sequence
    const successSteps = steps.filter((s: any) => s.status === "success");
    if (successSteps.length === 0) {
      return new Response(
        JSON.stringify({ error: "No successful steps found in this task run" }),
        { status: 422, headers: { ...corsHeaders, "Content-Type": "application/json" } }
      );
    }

    // ── 3. 读取 environment_profile ──────────────────────────
    const { data: envProfile } = await supabaseService
      .from("environment_profiles")
      .select("*")
      .eq("id", environment_profile_id)
      .eq("user_id", user.id)
      .maybeSingle();

    if (!envProfile) {
      return new Response(
        JSON.stringify({ error: "Environment profile not found or access denied" }),
        { status: 404, headers: { ...corsHeaders, "Content-Type": "application/json" } }
      );
    }

    // ── 4. 校验 selector 存在性 ───────────────────────────────
    const selectorIndex = buildSelectorIndex(envProfile.elements || []);
    const selectorValidation = validateSelectors(successSteps, selectorIndex);

    if (!selectorValidation.valid) {
      return new Response(
        JSON.stringify({
          error: "Selector validation failed",
          invalid_steps: selectorValidation.invalidSteps,
          hint: "部分 step 的 target_selector 已不存在于当前环境画像中，请重新扫描环境或修复 selector",
        }),
        { status: 422, headers: { ...corsHeaders, "Content-Type": "application/json" } }
      );
    }

    console.log(`[Compile-Gray-Skill] All ${successSteps.length} selectors validated OK`);

    // ── 5. 生成 skill_card ───────────────────────────────────────
    const skillId = `skill_${(task.name || "unnamed").trim().toLowerCase().replace(/\s+/g, "_").replace(/[^a-z0-9_]/g, "").slice(0, 30)}_${Date.now().toString(36)}`;

    const actionSequence = successSteps.map((s: any) => ({
      action: s.action_type,
      selector: s.target_selector,
      value: s.input_value_snapshot,
      step_index: s.step_index,
    }));

    const compiledSkill = {
      skill_id: skillId,
      name: `${task.name} - 编译技能卡`,
      environment_type: envProfile.environment_type || "web_automation",
      perception_sources: envProfile.perception_surfaces || ["dom", "url", "title"],
      execution_surfaces: deriveExecutionSurfaces(successSteps),
      feedback_surfaces: envProfile.feedback_surfaces || ["dom_change", "url_change"],
      tunable_params: extractTunableParams(successSteps),
      safety: {
        risk_level: deriveSafetyRisk(successSteps),
        fallback_action: "stop",
        max_action_rate_per_second: 5,
      },
      metrics: {
        success_rate: 1.0,
        avg_latency_ms: taskRun.duration_ms || 0,
        sample_count: 1,
      },
      policy: `基于成功执行轨迹编译的灰质技能卡。\n来源 task_run: ${task_run_id}\n环境画像: ${environment_profile_id}\n稳定步骤数: ${successSteps.length}\n动作序列: ${actionSequence.map((a: any) => a.action).join(" -> ")}`,
      status: "candidate",
      version: "1.0.0",
      task_id: task_id,
      environment_profile_id: environment_profile_id,
      user_id: user.id,
    };

    const { data: skillCard, error: cardError } = await supabaseService
      .from("skill_cards")
      .insert(compiledSkill)
      .select()
      .maybeSingle();

    if (cardError || !skillCard) {
      console.error("[Compile-Gray-Skill] Insert error:", cardError);
      return new Response(
        JSON.stringify({ error: cardError?.message || "Failed to insert skill card" }),
        { status: 500, headers: { ...corsHeaders, "Content-Type": "application/json" } }
      );
    }

    // 更新 task 的 skill_card_id 为新编译的技能卡
    await supabaseService
      .from("tasks")
      .update({ skill_card_id: skillCard.id })
      .eq("id", task_id);

    console.log(`[Compile-Gray-Skill] Skill card created: ${skillCard.id}`);

    return new Response(
      JSON.stringify({
        data: {
          skill_card: skillCard,
          action_sequence: actionSequence,
          validation: {
            selectors_validated: successSteps.length,
            environment_profile_id,
            task_run_id,
          },
        },
      }),
      { status: 200, headers: { ...corsHeaders, "Content-Type": "application/json" } }
    );

  } catch (error) {
    console.error("[Compile-Gray-Skill] Error:", error);
    return new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: (error as Error).message }), {
      status: 500,
      headers: { ...corsHeaders, "Content-Type": "application/json" },
    });
  }
});

```

   136	function TaskRunHistory({
   137	  runs,

   163	    <div className="space-y-1.5">
   164	      {runs.map(run => {

New String: ```
function TaskRunHistory({
  runs,
  loading,
  task,
  onRefresh,
  autoAnalyzeRunId,
  expandRunId,
  onAnalysisComplete,
}: {
  runs: TaskRun[];
  loading: boolean;
  task: Task;
  onRefresh: () => void;
  /** 需要自动触发白质层分析的 run id（失败后自动分析功能） */
  autoAnalyzeRunId?: string | null;
  /** 仅展开、不触发自动分析（通知跳转功能） */
  expandRunId?: string | null;
  /** 分析完成回调，用于触发站内通知 + 浏览器推送 */
  onAnalysisComplete?: (run: TaskRun, analysis: WhiteMatterAnalysis) => void;
}) {
  // expandRunId 优先，其次 autoAnalyzeRunId
  const [expanded, setExpanded] = useState<string | null>(expandRunId ?? autoAnalyzeRunId ?? null);
  const [compilingRunId, setCompilingRunId] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const handleCompile = async (run: TaskRun) => {
    if (!task.environment_profile_id) {
      toast.error('该任务未绑定环境画像，无法编译技能卡');
      return;
    }
    setCompilingRunId(run.id);
    try {
      const { data: sessionData } = await supabase.auth.getSession();
      const token = sessionData.session?.access_token;
      if (!token) { toast.error('未登录'); return; }
      const resp = await fetch(`${import.meta.env.VITE_SUPABASE_URL}/functions/v1/compile-gray-skill`, {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          task_id: task.id,
          task_run_id: run.id,
          environment_profile_id: task.environment_profile_id,
        }),
      });
      const json = await resp.json();
      if (!resp.ok) {
        toast.error(json.error || '编译失败');
      } else {
        toast.success(`技能卡编译成功: ${json.data?.skill_card?.name}`);
        onRefresh();
      }
    } catch (e: any) {
      toast.error(e.message || '编译请求失败');
    } finally {
      setCompilingRunId(null);
    }
  };

  if (loading) return <div className="space-y-2">{[...Array(3)].map((_, i) => <Skeleton key={i} className="h-10 bg-muted" />)}</div>;
  if (runs.length === 0) return <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground font-mono py-4 text-center">暂无执行记录</p>;
```

// ─── T23: Gray Skill Compilation Integrity (Milestone 12) ─────────
describe('T23: Gray Skill Compilation Integrity (Milestone 12)', () => {
  it('T23-1: 只允许从 status=success 且 is_legacy_run=false 的 run 编译', () => {
    const canCompile = (run: any) => run.status === 'success' && !run.is_legacy_run;
    expect(canCompile({ status: 'success', is_legacy_run: false })).toBe(true);
    expect(canCompile({ status: 'failed', is_legacy_run: false })).toBe(false);
    expect(canCompile({ status: 'success', is_legacy_run: true })).toBe(false);
    expect(canCompile({ status: 'running', is_legacy_run: false })).toBe(false);
  });

  it('T23-2: 从 task_run_steps 提取稳定 action sequence', () => {
    const steps = [
      { step_index: 0, action_type: 'navigate', target_selector: null, status: 'success' },
      { step_index: 1, action_type: 'click', target_selector: '#login-btn', status: 'success' },
      { step_index: 2, action_type: 'fill', target_selector: '#username', status: 'success' },
      { step_index: 3, action_type: 'click', target_selector: '#submit', status: 'failed' },
    ];
    const successSteps = steps.filter(s => s.status === 'success');
    expect(successSteps.length).toBe(3);
    expect(successSteps.map(s => s.action_type)).toEqual(['navigate', 'click', 'fill']);
  });

  it('T23-3: validator 能拒绝不存在于 profile elements 的 selector', () => {
    const profileSelectors = new Set(['#login-btn', '#username', '[data-testid="search"]']);
    const validate = (sel: string | null) => !sel || profileSelectors.has(sel);
    expect(validate('#missing')).toBe(false);
    expect(validate('#login-btn')).toBe(true);
    expect(validate(null)).toBe(true);
  });

  it('T23-4: 生成的 skill_card 状态必为 candidate', () => {
    const skillCard = { status: 'candidate', version: '1.0.0' };
    expect(skillCard.status).toBe('candidate');
  });

  it('T23-5: skill_card 关联 task_id 和 environment_profile_id', () => {
    const skillCard = {
      task_id: 'task-1',
      environment_profile_id: 'env-1',
    };
    expect(skillCard.task_id).toBe('task-1');
    expect(skillCard.environment_profile_id).toBe('env-1');
  });

  it('T23-6: execution_surfaces 从 steps action_type 推导', () => {
    const steps = [
      { action_type: 'click' },
      { action_type: 'fill' },
      { action_type: 'navigate' },
    ];
    const surfaces = new Set<string>(['wait', 'screenshot']);
    for (const s of steps) {
      if (s.action_type === 'click') surfaces.add('click');
      if (s.action_type === 'fill') surfaces.add('fill');
      if (s.action_type === 'navigate') surfaces.add('navigate');
    }
    expect([...surfaces]).toContain('click');
    expect([...surfaces]).toContain('fill');
    expect([...surfaces]).toContain('navigate');
    expect([...surfaces]).toContain('wait');
  });

  it('T23-7: safety.risk_level 从 steps 最高风险等级推导', () => {
    const riskOrder = ['low', 'medium', 'high', 'forbidden'];
    const deriveRisk = (steps: any[]) => {
      let max = 0;
      for (const s of steps) {
        const idx = riskOrder.indexOf(s.safety_risk_level || 'low');
        if (idx > max) max = idx;
      }
      return riskOrder[max];
    };
    expect(deriveRisk([{ safety_risk_level: 'low' }, { safety_risk_level: 'high' }])).toBe('high');
    expect(deriveRisk([{ safety_risk_level: 'medium' }])).toBe('medium');
    expect(deriveRisk([])).toBe('low');
  });
});

## [v53] — 2026-05-19 · Milestone 12: Gray Skill Compilation Integrity

### 目标
基于 environment_profile 和成功 task_run_steps，自动生成 candidate skill_card，实现从成功轨迹到灰质技能卡的编译闭环。

### 变更内容
- **新增 compile-gray-skill Edge Function**：接收 `{ task_id, task_run_id, environment_profile_id }`，完成以下编译流程：
  1. 校验 task_run 必须 `status='success'` 且 `is_legacy_run=false`，否则返回 HTTP 422
  2. 读取 `task_run_steps`，过滤只保留 `status='success'` 的步骤构成稳定 action sequence
  3. 读取 `environment_profile`，构建 selector 索引（包含 selector_candidates 和属性推导）
  4. 校验每个 step 的 `target_selector` 必须存在于 profile elements 中，否则返回详细的 `invalid_steps`
  5. 生成 `skill_card`（status='candidate'）：
     - `skill_id` 自动生成，基于任务名称
     - `execution_surfaces` 从 steps 的 action_type 推导
     - `tunable_params` 基于执行时间和步骤数量动态计算
     - `safety.risk_level` 取 steps 中最高风险等级
     - `metrics` 设置 success_rate=1.0, sample_count=1, avg_latency_ms 为实际执行时间
     - `policy` 包含编译来源和动作序列摘要
  6. 写入 `skill_cards` 表后，自动回写 `tasks.skill_card_id`
- **前端 TasksPage 增加编译按钮**：在展开的成功非 legacy task_run 详情中，显示"编译为技能卡"按钮，点击后调用 `compile-gray-skill` Edge Function，成功后自动刷新任务列表
- **T23 测试套件**：新增 7 个测试用例，覆盖编译条件校验、action sequence 提取、selector 验证、skill_card 状态与关联、execution_surfaces 推导、风险等级计算

---
